### [1] [Orchestrating Black-Box Schema Converters: An Empirical Study of Automated, Quality-Ranked Conversion Across Heterogeneous Schema Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26180)
*Felix Neubauer,Giridhar Chinnikkaramadom Govindan,Jürgen Pleiss,Benjamin Uekermann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模式转换编排方法，将不同模式语言间的转换器视为有向边，通过路径发现、执行、排序和全步骤溯源，实现自动化、可复现、质量排序的模式转换，并在60个真实任务中取得43个可用结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常需在同一数据模型的不同模式语言表示（如JSON Schema、XSD、SHACL）间保持一致，但现有转换器难以寻找、分布零散、质量参差不齐且常有信息丢失，给模型演化的构建和维护带来沉重负担。

Method: 将模式语言建模为节点，转换器（视为黑盒）建模为有向边，转换路径通过图搜索发现、执行、排序并报告完整的每步溯源信息，失败时尝试替代路径；实现为开源工具Schema Conversion Orchestrator，集成到MetaConfigurator中。

Result: 在涵盖5种模式语言的60个真实转换任务上评估，编排方法为43个任务提供了可用结果，剩余失败任务定位了当前转换器生态的具体缺口。

Conclusion: 编排方法能有效整合现有异构转换器以自动化模式转换，但仍存在生态缺口；研究对工具构建者和转换质量评估具有启示意义。

Abstract: Modern software systems routinely need the same data model in several schema languages: a model may exist as JSON Schema for a web API, as XSD for data exchange, and as SHACL for a knowledge graph. Keeping these representations consistent as the model evolves is a recurring construction and maintenance burden, because converters between schema languages are hard to find, scattered across ecosystems, of uneven quality, and frequently lossy. We study, empirically, to what extent such imperfect, heterogeneous converters can be orchestrated into automated, reproducible, and quality-ranked conversions, and where the current converter landscape reaches its limits. Our approach models schema languages as nodes and converters, treated as black boxes, as directed edges, so that conversions become paths that are discovered, executed, ranked, and reported with full per-step provenance, with failures handled by trying alternatives. We realize it as the open-source Schema Conversion Orchestrator, integrate it into MetaConfigurator, and evaluate it on 60 conversion tasks built from real-world schemas across five schema languages, using agent-assisted, human-reviewed quality annotations. Orchestration surfaces a usable result for 43 of 60 tasks; the remaining failures localize concrete gaps in the converter landscape. We discuss implications for tool builders and for measuring conversion quality.

</details>


### [2] [Augmentation with Dilution: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Human Contributor Ecosystems After AI Coding Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26289)
*Weixing Zhang,Bowen Jiang,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用2023年1月至2026年5月期间11,097个GitHub仓库的数据，采用交错双重差分法发现：AI编码代理的采用并未显著改变人类贡献者的绝对数量，但显著降低了人类贡献者密度（-1.9个百分点），新人相对参与份额下降3.7个百分点，而评审深度增加5.3%，呈现出"增强与稀释并存"的模式——AI并未取代人类，但系统性地重塑了开源生态的参与结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将人类贡献者视为静态背景，缺乏从人类贡献者生态系统角度考察AI编码代理影响的大规模实证研究。

Method: 采用交错双重差分设计（Staggered DiD），使用Sun and Abraham估计量，对2023年1月至2026年5月间11,097个GitHub仓库进行因果推断分析。

Result: (1) 人类贡献者绝对数量无显著变化（ATT=0.014, p=0.224）；(2) 人类贡献者密度显著下降（ATT=-0.019, p=0.002）；(3) 新人相对参与份额显著下降3.7个百分点（ATT=-0.037, p<0.001）；(4) 评审深度显著增加5.3%（ATT=+0.0168, p<0.001）；(5) 效应受项目规模、编程语言和项目成熟度调节。

Conclusion: AI编码代理并未替代人类贡献者，但通过稀释人类参与密度、抑制新人参与、加重评审负担的方式，系统性地重塑了开源生态系统的参与结构，呈现"增强与稀释并存"的特征。

Abstract: AI coding agents are penetrating open-source software development at an unprecedented pace, yet existing research predominantly treats human contributors as a static backdrop rather than as the subject of inquiry. This paper presents the first large-scale empirical study that takes the human contributor ecosystem as its dependent variable, examining how the number, composition, and behavior of human participants change following AI coding agent adoption in open-source projects. Using a staggered difference-in-differences design on a dataset of 11,097 GitHub repositories spanning January 2023 to May 2026, we provide causal evidence via the Sun and Abraham estimator. Our results show that AI agent adoption does not significantly change the absolute number of human contributors (ATT = 0.014, p = 0.224), but significantly reduces human contributor density (ATT = -0.019, p = 0.002), indicating that the relative share of human participation declines as AI-generated pull requests accumulate. The relative participation share of newcomers declines significantly by 3.7 percentage points (ATT = -0.037, p < 0.001), with the effect emerging immediately after adoption and remaining stable throughout the observation window. Review depth increases significantly by 5.3% (ATT = +0.0168, p < 0.001), indicating that AI agents shift burden from the code production stage to the review stage. Moderator analysis reveals that these effects vary systematically with project size, programming language, and project maturity. Together, these findings present a pattern of augmentation with dilution: AI agents are not displacing human contributors, but are systematically reshaping the participation structure of open-source ecosystems.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Safety-Aware Mutation Testing for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26456)
*Donghwan Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出安全感知变异测试（SAMT），通过在ADS模块间消息中注入时间受限故障来模拟交互失败，并基于STPA等安全工程框架推导变异规则，以实现更系统的测试充分性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADS测试缺乏何时停止生成测试场景的系统性准则；传统覆盖度量无法捕捉导致大多数事故的组件交互故障；传统变异测试直接应用于ADS模块（代码级或深度学习模型级）效果不足。

Method: 提出SAMT范式：不向单个组件注入人工故障，而是在ADS模块间交换的消息中注入时间受限故障；从STPA等自上而下的安全工程框架中推导变异生成规则；将系统思维嵌入变异测试流程。

Result: SAMT提供了一种严格的机制来评估测试充分性，支持自动化场景生成，并指导ADS修复。论文同时概述了关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: SAMT通过系统性的安全感知变异方法，有望弥补传统变异测试和覆盖度量在ADS交互故障检测上的不足，为ADS测试充分性评估提供新方向。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is essential for ensuring the safety of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS), yet the community lacks a systematic criterion for determining when we can safely stop additional test scenario generation. Existing coverage metrics typically focus on individual component reliability or treat the ADS as a black box, failing to capture certain component interactions that cause most ADS accidents. While traditional mutation testing provides a falsifiable measure of test adequacy, directly porting code- and deep learning model-level mutations to the corresponding modules of ADS is insufficient.
  In this vision paper, we propose a paradigm shift toward Safety-Aware Mutation Testing (SAMT). Unlike traditional mutation testing, which creates mutants (i.e., faulty versions of the software under test) by injecting artificial faults into individual components, SAMT systematically injects temporally bounded faults into the messages exchanged between ADS modules to simulate realistic interaction failures. To ensure these mutants represent genuine hazards, we propose deriving mutant generation rules directly from top-down safety engineering frameworks, such as System-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). By embedding systems thinking into the mutation testing pipeline, SAMT provides a rigorous mechanism for evaluating test adequacy, enabling automated scenario generation, and guiding ADS repair. We also outline critical open challenges.

</details>


### [4] [An Empirical Study of LLM-Generated Specifications for VeriFast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26490)
*Wen Fan,Minh Tran,Sanya Dod,Xin Hu,Marilyn Rego,Danning Xie,Jenna DiVincenzo,Lin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在生成分离逻辑（SL）验证器VeriFast规范方面的能力，对303个C函数采用8种提示方法、10种LLMs和3种输入类型进行两阶段实验。结果表明LLMs在保持功能行为方面表现良好（代码和规范均超过91%），但验证成功率仅31.4%，且94%的错误源于LLMs对SL验证器领域知识的欠缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态验证工具（尤其是基于分离逻辑的验证器）需要大量人工编写复杂辅助规范，而现有LLM工作主要针对非SL验证器，缺乏对SL验证器规范生成能力的系统评估。

Method: 对303个C函数，采用8种提示方法、10种LLMs和3种输入类型，分两阶段进行实验，通过定量和定性分析评估LLM生成的代码和规范的功能行为、可验证性和错误类型。

Result: LLMs生成的代码和规范功能保持率均超过91%，但验证成功率仅31.4%；Gemini 2.5 Pro和提供形式化契约可获得更高成功率；94%的错误来自LLMs对SL验证器领域知识的错误。

Conclusion: LLMs在生成SL验证器规范方面仍有显著不足，主要瓶颈在于对SL领域知识的掌握，研究结果为优化LLM生成的SL验证器规范提供了指导。

Abstract: Static verification tools can assure industrial scale software, but require significant human labor to write specifications. This is particularly true of static verifiers based on separation logic (SL verifiers), which excel at verifying heapmanipulating programs, but require many complex auxiliary specifications to reason about heap structure. Recent work applies large language models (LLMs) to generate code, tests, and proofs, including specifications for verifiers, but mostly targeting non-SL verifiers. To address this gap, this paper thoroughly evaluates how well LLMs perform when prompted to generate specifications for verifying 303 C functions with the SL verifier VeriFast. We explored eight prompting approaches, ten LLMs, and three input types in two stages. Quantitative and qualitative analyses are used to assess the LLM-generated code and specifications for functional behavior, verifiability and errors. The results show that LLMs preserve functional behavior in source code and specifications (both over 91%), but achieve modest verification success (31.4%). Using Gemini 2.5 Pro and providing formal contracts lead to higher success rates in our setting. Moreover, most errors (94%) come from LLMs' mistakes in the domainspecific knowledge of SL verifiers such as VeriFast. These findings provide guidance for optimizing LLM-generated specifications for SL verifiers.

</details>


### [5] [Evaluation-Strategy Gap in Fault Diagnosis of Deep Learning Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26492)
*Sigma Jahan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现现有深度学习故障诊断技术在跨程序评估中存在0.190的平衡准确率差距，归因于程序级特征结构；曲率特征对未见程序的故障检测有效，而优化器和激活特征仅对训练中见过的程序有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习程序训练失败诊断技术通常采用程序内交叉验证评估，但这种方法可能不适用于涉及未见过程序的部署场景，因此需要评估不同场景下的性能差异并识别导致性能差距的原因。

Method: 使用DynFault语料库（包含38个真实DL程序的5,542个故障注入训练轨迹），对比程序内评估与留出完整程序评估的性能差异，并分析曲率特征、优化器特征和激活特征在不同评估场景下的表现。

Result: 现有故障诊断技术在程序内评估与留出完整程序评估之间的平衡准确率差距为0.190；曲率特征对未见程序的不稳定性检测有效，而优化器和激活特征仅在训练见过的程序上有帮助。

Conclusion: 程序级特征结构是导致跨程序评估性能差距的主要原因，不同特征集在泛化能力上存在显著差异，曲率特征更具泛化性。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) programs can fail during training for many reasons, and diagnosing the cause is a costly and time-consuming maintenance task. Techniques for diagnosing such failures are commonly assessed using within-program cross-validation, which may be inadequate for deployment settings involving previously unseen programs. It is therefore necessary to assess how performance differs across these settings and to identify the causes of any performance gap in established fault diagnosis techniques for DL. We investigate this gap using DynFault, a corpus of 5,542 fault-injected training traces from 38 real-world DL programs. We found a gap of 0.190 in balanced accuracy for existing fault diagnosis techniques between within-program evaluation and holding out whole programs. We also found the gap comes from program-level structure in the features, which led us to examine two runtime feature sets, curvature features and optimizer features, and their behavior on unseen programs. We found that curvature features are useful for instability detection on unseen programs, while optimizer and activation features help only on programs seen during training.

</details>


### [6] [Same Scrutiny, More Time: Eye Tracking Insights into Reviewing LLM-Labelled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26505)
*Ranim Khojah,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Mazen Mohamad,Julian Frattini,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过Wizard-of-Oz实验发现，虽然明确标注为LLM生成的代码标签并未改变开发者代码审查的彻底性，但会增加他们对代码的注视时间，并促使他们调整审查策略（如关注逻辑正确性、利用提示词引导审查），揭示了审查者意图与实际行为之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码仍存在错误和幻觉，需要仔细审查，但开发者对LLM生成代码的实际审查行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用Wizard-of-Oz实验设计，通过眼动追踪和退出访谈收集行为数据和参与者反馈，结合贝叶斯数据分析和定性分析进行研究。

Result: 代码审查的彻底性未因LLM标签而改变，但参与者对标注为LLM生成的代码注视时间更长；开发者会根据特定标准（如逻辑正确性）调整审查策略，并使用提示词引导审查。

Conclusion: LLM标签会影响开发者的注意力分配和审查策略，但未提升审查彻底性；需在设计LLM工具时纳入提示词作为软件制品，并建议软件公司重新审视AI政策以更好支持LLM代码审查。

Abstract: Modern software development increasingly involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to generate code. Despite their rapid advancement, LLMs remain prone to errors and hallucinations, emphasizing the importance of careful code inspection. However, in practice, developers' trust in LLM-generated code and their willingness to review it thoroughly may differ from these recommendations. How developers actually behave when reviewing LLM-generated code remains largely unexplored. In this study, we conduct a Wizard-of-Oz experiment to examine how software engineers behave when code is explicitly labeled as LLM-generated during a code review task. We collect both behavioral data and participant feedback through eye-tracking and exit interviews. Combining Bayesian data analysis with qualitative analysis, we found that while the thoroughness of code review did not change for participants, they spent more time fixating on LLM-labelled code, indicating that the label itself influences attention. Practitioners also adapted their review strategy for LLM-labelled code by assessing the code based on specific criteria (e.g., logical correctness), or using the prompt to guide their review. These findings inform LLM-based tool design on labelling while incorporating the prompt as a software artifact. Our study reveals a gap between reviewers' intentions and actual reviewing behaviour, highlighting the need for software companies to revisit their AI policies (particularly regarding LLM-assisted development) to better support developers in reviewing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [7] [ConcoLixir: Reactive LLM Discovery Oracles for Python Concolic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26545)
*Dong Chen,Chih-Duo Hong,Fang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConcoLixir是一个结合LLM的Python concolic测试扩展，通过LLM作为发现预言机（生成种子、处理求解失败、覆盖停滞问题），在无需替换符号求解器的前提下，在合成/真实/库目标上分别提升行覆盖率8.6、15.1、17.0个百分点，总API成本仅1.63美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python程序的concolic测试面临三重限制：库调用导致符号变量降级为具体值；正则表达式、校验和、解析器等语义操作难以求解；探索会在已覆盖路径上停滞不前。

Method: ConcoLixir将LLM作为发现预言机集成到Python concolic执行中。LLM不替代符号求解器或正确性预言机，而是执行三个具体任务：生成初始种子、在求解器失败后提出具体输入、在覆盖率停滞时针对未覆盖代码生成输入。每个候选输入被concolically执行，仅凭观察到的覆盖率和收集的路径约束指导后续探索。

Result: 在合成、真实项目和库目标上，ConcoLixir相比基线concolic测试器分别提升平均行覆盖率8.6、15.1和17.0个百分点。提升在语义障碍和库边界处最显著，完整评估的API费用仅为1.63美元。

Conclusion: 有边界限制的LLM发现能力可以补充符号推理，而无需替代它，在成本极低的情况下有效克服concolic测试在语义屏障和库边界处的固有局限。

Abstract: Concolic testing combines concrete execution with symbolic constraint solving, but Python programs expose recurring limits. Library calls can cause symbolic variables to downgrade to concrete values. Regular expressions, checksums, parsers, and other semantic operations can be hard to solve, and exploration can plateau on already covered paths. We present ConcoLixir, a reactive LLM extension for Python concolic execution. The LLM acts as a discovery oracle, not a replacement for the solver or a correctness oracle. It generates initial seeds, proposes concrete inputs after solver failures, and targets uncovered code when coverage stalls. Each candidate is executed concolically, and only observed coverage and collected path constraints guide later exploration. Across synthetic, real-world, and library targets, ConcoLixir improves mean line coverage over the baseline concolic tester without an LLM oracle by 8.6, 15.1, and 17.0 percentage points. The gains are strongest near semantic barriers and library boundaries, and the full evaluation costs \$1.63 in API charges. These results show that bounded LLM discovery can complement symbolic reasoning without replacing it.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum Mutant Equivalence via Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26604)
*José Campos,Andriy Miranskyy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于转译器（Transpiler）的轻量级方法TBE，用于识别量子软件变异测试中的等价变异体，通过比较原始和变异量子电路在相同配置下转译后的OpenQASM代码来实现，在348,299个存活变异体上达到100%精度和82%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件中变异测试面临等价变异体问题，未被识别的等价变异体会浪费测试资源并扭曲变异得分；已有研究生成大量量子电路变异体后发现约半数存活，但无法判断这些存活变异体是源于弱测试还是语义等价。

Method: 在相同配置下对原始电路和变异电路进行转译（transpile），然后比较二者生成的OpenQASM代码是否一致，若一致则判定变异体为等价变异体。

Result: 在348,299个存活变异体中识别出29,536个等价变异体（占32.1%），达到了100%的精度和82%的准确率。

Conclusion: TBE是一种轻量级且高效的方法，能够高精度地识别量子软件中的等价变异体，有助于提升量子变异测试的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test suite quality by introducing artificial faults (mutants) and checking whether tests detect (kill) them. A central challenge is the equivalent mutant problem: some mutants are syntactically different from the original program but semantically identical to it and therefore cannot be killed by any test. If left unidentified, such mutants waste testing effort and distort mutation scores. In quantum software, mutation testing is increasingly used, but the equivalent mutant problem remains unsolved. A recent study generated more than 700,000 quantum circuit mutants and found that roughly half survived the available tests, making it unclear whether these survivors reflect weak tests or semantic equivalence. We propose Transpiler-Based Equivalence (TBE), a lightweight approach that identifies equivalent quantum mutants by transpiling original and mutated circuits under the same configuration and comparing their resulting OpenQASM code. We evaluate TBE on 348,299 surviving mutants, 92,011 of which are equivalent; TBE identifies 29,536 of them (32.1%) as equivalent while achieving 100% precision and 82% accuracy.

</details>


### [9] [Knowledge-Based Pull Requests: A Trusted Workflow for Agent-Mediated Knowledge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26721)
*Xinyu Zhang,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出知识驱动拉取请求（KPR）工作流，将外部协作者的代码、测试及Agent交互痕迹视为知识源而非默认合并候选，通过项目内部可信Agent在项目环境下重新生成代码，从而将“知识是否应进入项目”与“特定实现是否应合并”两个决策解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码Agent正在改变软件协作的瓶颈：代码越来越廉价，而理解意图、协商范围、管理长期项目责任仍然成本高昂。传统PR流程将这两个决策混为一谈，导致跨信任边界（开源、企业、供应商、承包商、客户驱动场景）的协作效率低下。

Method: KPR工作流：外部协作者的知识源被Agent提炼为人类确认的知识包，呈现为设计备忘录、风险检查清单、测试计划或实现简报等审阅者友好形式；项目内部的可信Agent在项目环境下重新生成候选代码。包含候选构件模式、成本核算视图、协作网关架构。

Result: 在7个已合并的公开PR上进行了受控模拟实验，验证了KPR包可以从真实PR材料中实例化，并在描述消融、差异消融和合成毒化补丁条件下进行压力测试。

Conclusion: KPR定位为可实验验证的工作流，其价值取决于可审计的提取、转换和项目侧再生是否能够降低理解与重做高上下文外部变更的成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents are changing the bottleneck in software collaboration: code is increasingly cheap, while understanding intent, negotiating scope, and governing long-term project responsibility remain costly. This paper proposes \emph{Knowledge-Based Pull Requests} (KPR), a trusted workflow for agent-mediated software collaboration across trust boundaries, including open source, enterprise, vendor, contractor, and customer-driven settings. In KPR, an external collaborator's local code, tests, and cleaned agent interaction trace are treated as knowledge sources rather than as the default merge candidate. Agents distill these sources into a human-confirmed knowledge package and render it into reviewer-facing forms such as design memos, risk checklists, test plans, or implementation briefs. A project-owned inner trusted coding agent then regenerates candidate code inside the receiving project's environment under repository context, engineering conventions, tests, and security policy. KPR therefore separates two decisions that traditional pull requests often collapse: whether the knowledge should enter the project, and whether a particular implementation should be merged. We contribute the KPR workflow, a candidate artifact schema, a cost-accounting view, a collaboration gateway architecture, a minimal controlled simulation pilot over seven merged public pull requests, and an evaluation agenda. The pilot shows that KPR packages can be instantiated from real PR material and stress-tested under description ablation, diff ablation, and synthetic poisoned-patch conditions. We position KPR as an empirically testable workflow: its value depends on whether auditable extraction, transformation, and project-side regeneration reduce the cost of understanding and reworking high-context external changes.

</details>


### [10] [A Deterministic Control Plane for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26924)
*Padmaraj Madatha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对10,008个GitHub仓库的实证研究发现AI agent配置文件存在大规模未管理传播（10.1%重复、跨组织克隆、低修订率、权限声明不足），据此提出确定性控制平面Rel(AI)Build，通过内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、层级权限、攻击黑名单、阶段状态机、多IDE编译和提示漂移检测等机制治理配置层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码工具赋予agent广泛的文件和shell访问权限，但配置层（规则文件、agent定义、IDE特定markdown）缺乏有效管理，存在安全隐患。

Method: 1) 对10,008个公共GitHub仓库（含6,145个agent配置文件）进行大规模实证研究，分析配置文件的重复率、跨组织传播、修订频率和权限声明情况；2) 提出Rel(AI)Build系统，包含SHA-256内容寻址、HMAC锁文件、哈希链审计日志、层级权限、攻击衍生的黑名单、阶段状态机、多IDE编译和Jaccard相似度提示漂移检测等确定性机制。

Result: 10.1%的跟踪路径在不同仓库间是SHA-256精确重复，75.5%的克隆对跨组织边界；58%的配置仅有一次提交，权限声明的agent配置不足1%（对比Actions工作流的33%）。Rel(AI)Build的符合性测试确认各机制能执行其声明的约束。

Conclusion: agent配置层存在系统性治理空白，需要确定性的、工具无关的控制平面，而非将治理委托给进一步的LLM编排。开发者实际使用效果有待未来研究。

Abstract: LLM coding harnesses grant agents broad file and shell access, yet the configuration layer that steers them -- rules files, agent definitions, IDE-specific markdown -- is largely unmanaged. A prevalence study of 10,008 public GitHub repositories (n=6,145 agent config files) finds that agent configurations propagate as undeclared shared components: 10.1% of tracked paths are SHA-256 exact duplicates across independent repositories (fork-adjusted, threshold-independent), with 75.5% of clone pairs crossing organisational boundaries. Two further patterns are indicative: configurations are rarely revised (58% single-commit; 0.4 vs 0.6 commits/month age-normalised against CI/CD workflows), and rarely declare permission boundaries (<1% of agent configs vs 33% of Actions workflows, n=31 true positives).
  We propose a deterministic control plane above the harness that maps one-to-one to these gaps. Rel(AI)Build treats agent definitions as a managed supply chain (SHA-256 content addressing, HMAC-stamped lockfiles, hash-chained audit logs); enforces tiered permissions and attack-derived blocklists before LLM invocation; gates feature work through a phase state machine with requirement-to-file-to-test traceability; compiles a single canonical definition to seven IDE targets; and detects prompt drift via Jaccard similarity. Conformance tests on injected violations confirm each mechanism enforces its stated invariant; developer outcomes remain future work. Governance of this layer must be deterministic and tool-agnostic -- not delegated to further LLM orchestration.

</details>


### [11] [Are LLMs Ready for Anti-Pattern Detection in Microservice Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26927)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Amalfitano,Porfirio Tramontana,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）通过基于提示的分析流程，在微服务架构中检测架构反模式的能力，并与静态分析工具MARS进行了对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中频繁出现的架构反模式会阻碍系统的可维护性、可演化性和运行质量。现有检测方法依赖静态分析和手工规则，存在工具依赖性强、检测逻辑固化、难以适应异构仓库等局限性，亟需探索更灵活高效的检测手段。

Method: 构建了一个包含16种架构反模式标注的微服务仓库基准数据集，采用基于提示的分析流程，利用三种通用LLMs对静态仓库制品进行分析，并与静态分析工具MARS在精确率和召回率指标上进行统一协议评估。

Result: LLMs在多种反模式检测上表现出竞争力，特别是在证据具有局部性、异构性或语义丰富性的场景下；但在需要显式结构或跨服务依赖证据的反模式上表现有限，静态分析仍更为可靠。

Conclusion: LLMs尚不能替代传统静态分析工具，但已展现出作为微服务架构评估中互补辅助手段的潜力。

Abstract: Microservice systems are prone to recurrent architectural anti-patterns (APs) that hinder maintainability, evolvability, and operational quality. Most existing AP detection approaches rely on static analysis and handcrafted rules, which can be effective but are often tool-dependent, limited to explicitly encoded detection logic, and difficult to adapt to heterogeneous repositories. In this paper, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) are ready to support architectural anti-pattern detection in microservice architectures through a prompt-based analysis pipeline over static repository artifacts. We evaluate three general-purpose LLMs on a curated benchmark of microservice repositories annotated with 16 architectural anti-patterns, and compare their performance against the state-of-the-art static-analysis tool MARS using a uniform evaluation protocol based on precision and recall. Our results show that LLMs can provide useful support for anti-pattern detection, achieving competitive performance on several anti-patterns, especially when the relevant evidence is local, heterogeneous, or semantically rich. At the same time, they exhibit clear limitations on anti-patterns that require explicit structural or cross-service dependency evidence, where static analysis remains more reliable. These findings suggest that LLMs are not yet a replacement for traditional analyzers, but already represent a promising complementary aid for architectural assessment in microservice systems.

</details>


### [12] [CLIR: Liveness-Driven and Structure-Aware Fuzzing for the Cranelift Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26977)
*Shangtong Cao,Tianlei Song,Qiuping Yi,Tianyu Chen,Guoai Xu,Ningyu He,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIR，一种针对Cranelift后端编译器的高效差分测试框架，通过语法保持的层次化生成、活跃度引导的指令优化和跨架构诊断适配机制，在72小时内发现24个跨架构bug，检测效率显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cranelift作为新兴编译器后端日益重要，但现有编译器测试方法难以应对其特殊挑战：SSA形式下构造有效IR困难、难以生成高计算密度的序列以充分测试后端组件、以及跨异构架构的广泛后端覆盖与高效根因分析难以兼顾。

Method: 提出CLIR差分测试框架，包含三种核心机制：(1) 语法保持的层次化生成策略，保证SSA形式的合法性；(2) 活跃度引导的指令精炼机制，最大化计算密度；(3) 诊断引导的跨架构适配方案，促进异构后端的根因分析。

Result: CLIR检测到的独特bug数量是cranelift-fuzzgen的8倍、wasm-smith的24倍、WASMaker的8倍，RustSmith未发现任何bug。72小时内发现24个覆盖所有目标架构的bug，其中21个已确认、9个已修复。

Conclusion: CLIR在Cranelift编译器后端的差分测试中显著优于现有最先进基线方法，能有效发现跨异构架构的实际bug。

Abstract: Modern compilers are complex software systems that must correctly translate high-level programming languages into machine code across multiple architectures. Cranelift, a fast and modern compiler backend originally developed for WebAssembly and recently adopted as an experimental backend for Rust, has gained increasing importance due to its superior compilation speed compared to LLVM and comprehensive multi-architecture support, including x86-64, AArch64, s390x, and RISCV64. However, despite decades of development in compiler testing, testing Cranelift still presents unique challenges, including (1) constructing valid IR under the strict enforcement of SSA form, (2) generating sequences with sufficient computational density to stress backend components, and (3) balancing broad backend coverage with efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous architectures.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLIR, a differential testing framework that integrates a syntax-preserving hierarchical generation strategy to guarantee SSA validity, a liveness-guided instruction refinement mechanism to maximize computational density, and a diagnosis-guided cross-architecture adaptation scheme to facilitate efficient root cause analysis across heterogeneous backends. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that CLIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, detecting 8x, 24x, and 8x more unique bugs than cranelift-fuzzgen, wasm-smith, and WASMaker, respectively, while RustSmith uncovered no bugs. Consequently, within 72 hours of testing, CLIR discovered 24 bugs spanning all target architectures, with 21 confirmed and 9 fixed.

</details>


### [13] [To Run or Not to Run: Analyzing the Cost-Effectiveness of Code Execution in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26978)
*Zhihao Lin,Junhua Zhu,Mingyi Zhou,Xin Wang,Zhensu Sun,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段实证研究分析了LLM修复代理中代码执行行为的影响，发现：执行使用普遍但行为差异大；限制执行对修复成功率影响很小（仅1.25个百分点差异），但可大幅节省成本；执行收益集中在少数实例上，当前代理存在无差别使用执行的问题，应将执行视为需权衡成本收益的资源而非默认能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于"生成-运行-修正"范式的LLM修复代理虽已成为标准实践，但代码执行耗时且昂贵，其对这些代理的实际影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 两阶段实证研究：阶段一分析SWE-bench排行榜中7,745条代理轨迹以刻画大规模执行行为；阶段二在200个SWE-bench实例上评估三种代理（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode）在四种执行范式下的3,000次端到端修复尝试，进行性能和成本的细粒度比较。

Result: (1)所有代理和模型均使用代码执行，平均每任务8.8次测试运行，执行频率范围2-19次，后期执行成功率高于早期；(2)执行限制对修复成功影响很小，商业代理上禁止执行与无限制之间仅1.25个百分点差异且不显著，但大幅节省token和耗时；(3)执行收益集中而非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 当前代理无差别使用执行，在收益甚微的实例上支付了成本。执行应被视为具有明确成本效益权衡的资源，而非默认能力。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for program repair are increasingly built on a "generate-run-revise" paradigm, iteratively executing tests to evaluate and refine patches. This execution-based approach has become standard practice in state-of-the-art systems. However, executions can be time-consuming and expensive, yet their impact on these agents remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a two-stage empirical study over execution behavior in LLM-based program repair. To characterize execution behavior at scale, we first analyze 7,745 agent traces from SWE-bench leaderboard submissions. Second, we evaluate 3,000 end-to-end repair attempts across 200 SWE-bench instances and three agents (Claude Code, Codex, and the open-source OpenCode) under four execution paradigms, which allows for a fine-grained comparison of performance and cost. Our analysis reveals three key observations: (1) Code execution is used across all agents and models analyzed, with an average of 8.8 test runs per task. Execution behavior varies substantially across agents and models, with frequency ranging from 2 to 19 per task, and late-stage executions consistently achieve higher success rates than early-stage ones. (2) Execution restrictions have little effect on repair success: on commercial agents with SOTA models the resolve-rate gap between Prohibited and Unrestricted is only 1.25 percentage points and not statistically significant, while Prohibited saves substantial token and wall-clock cost. (3) Execution benefit is concentrated rather than uniform. These patterns suggest that current agents apply execution indiscriminately, paying its cost on instances where it provides little benefit. Execution, therefore, should be treated as a resource with an explicit cost-benefit tradeoff, not a default capability.

</details>


### [14] [How Much Static Structure Do Code Agents Need? A Study of Deterministic Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26979)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Yizhuo Yang,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究轻量级静态分析（如调用图、继承层次等结构注释）能否为基于LLM的代码代理提供确定性锚点，使其导航更稳定可复现。实验表明，结构注释虽不能使代理更"聪明"，但能使其导航更规范：提升了定位精度（Func@5+2.2pp）、缩短了交互轨迹（-1.6轮）、降低了运行方差（减半），代价是约10%的额外token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码代理通常依赖关键词搜索进行仓库导航，忽略了调用图、继承层次和配置依赖等结构关系，导致导航随机性强、难以复现。作者希望探索轻量级静态分析能否提供确定性锚点，使代理导航更可预测。

Method: 基于OpenAI的Codex基线，系统性地注入不同粒度的结构注释（调用图、继承拓扑等），测量对定位、轨迹行为和运行间稳定性的影响，分析"确定性锚定效应"。

Result: (1) 锚定有效：轻量级调用/继承拓扑使Func@5提升2.2pp，交互轮数减少1.6轮；(2) 锚定对规模敏感：最优粒度和方向性取决于仓库特征，密集语义呈现边际递减，中心化项目受益于反向链接；(3) 锚定稳定：链接跟踪率从0.15-0.18提升至0.21-0.24，方差减半，Pass@1提升3.4pp，但增加约10% token开销。

Conclusion: 提出了实用指南：中型项目默认使用轻量级拓扑，大型仓库剪除前向边，隐式依赖场景保留密集标签。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents navigate repositories through keyword search but miss the structural relationships, such as call graphs, inheritance hierarchies, and configuration dependencies, that define how software actually works. This makes agent navigation stochastic and difficult to reproduce across runs. We investigate whether lightweight static analysis can provide deterministic anchors for these agents: stable structural facts injected as plain-text comments that constrain probabilistic exploration and make navigation more predictable. Starting from a strong baseline, Codex from OpenAI, we systematically inject varying granularities of structural annotations and measure their effects on localization, trajectory behavior, and run-to-run stability. Our study identifies what we call the deterministic anchoring effect: static structure helps less by making agents "smarter" and more by making their navigation disciplined and reproducible. Three observations support this finding: (1) Anchoring works: lightweight call/inheritance topology improves function-level localization (+2.2pp Func@5) and shortens trajectories (-1.6 interaction rounds); (2) Anchoring is scale-sensitive: the optimal granularity and directionality depend on repository characteristics, where denser semantics show diminishing returns and hub-heavy projects benefit from inverse-only links that expose "who-calls-me" without forward edges; (3) Anchoring stabilizes: tags raise link-following rate from 0.15-0.18 to 0.21-0.24, roughly halve run-to-run variance, and improve single-run reliability (Pass@1 +3.4 pp) on medium-scale repositories, at the cost of roughly 10% more input tokens. These observations suggest practical guidelines: default to lightweight topology on medium projects, prune forward edges in large repositories, and reserve dense tags for implicit-dependency cases.

</details>


### [15] [Cleaning Logs for Downstream Tasks (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27000)
*Zahra G. Yazdi,Van-Hoang Le,Nyyti Saarimäki,Donghwan Shin,Domenico Bianculli,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogPurifier，一种基于日志模板间依赖关系的任务无关日志清洗方法，旨在识别并移除与系统功能行为无关的游离消息，提升下游分析任务（模型推理与异常检测）的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际日志中包含大量冗余和不相关信息（游离消息），这些信息会负面影响下游分析任务（如模型推理和异常检测）的性能，因此需要一种有效的日志清洗方法。

Method: 提出LogPurifier方法，基于日志消息模板之间的依赖关系识别并移除游离消息，该方法具有任务无关性（task-agnostic）。

Result: 论文计划通过受控实验设计进行实证评估，考察LogPurifier对模型推理和异常检测两项下游任务在有效性和效率方面的影响。

Conclusion: LogPurifier通过依赖关系分析实现任务无关的日志清洗，有望在提升下游任务性能的同时保持通用性，但结论尚待实证评估验证。

Abstract: Background: Software systems generate logs during execution to record critical events and runtime information for troubleshooting and monitoring. However, in practice, logs often contain significant amounts of redundant and irrelevant information, which can negatively impact the performance of downstream analysis tasks, such as model inference and anomaly detection. Objective: The objective of this study is to clean log data by identifying and removing free-standing messages -- messages that are not relevant to the execution behaviors of interest and are interleaved with messages capturing the system's functional behavior. Method: To address this objective, we propose LogPurifier, a task-agnostic log-cleaning approach based on dependency relationships between log message templates. The paper presents a plan for an empirical evaluation using a controlled experimental design to assess the impact of LogPurifier on the effectiveness and efficiency of two downstream tasks: model inference and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [16] [The Spec Growth Engine: Spec-Anchored, Code-Coupled, Drift-Enforced Architecture for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27045)
*Hartwig Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Spec Growth Engine，一个轻量级框架，通过机器可读的规范图、Spine上下文组装器、垂直切片增长协议和漂移门机制，解决AI编码代理在加速开发时面临的上下文爆炸和规范-代码静默漂移两个结构性失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理虽然大幅提升了实现速度，但引入两个现有规范驱动方法未完全解决的结构性失效模式：(1)上下文爆炸——代理需要同时推理整个仓库，上下文窗口填满后输出质量下降；(2)规范-代码静默漂移——代码不断演化而规范未同步更新，当差异变得可见时修复成本已很高。

Method: 提出Spec Growth Engine框架，包含四个核心组件：(1)机器可读的规范图，节点携带明确的契约/设计分离；(2)Spine上下文组装器，将代理上下文限定在所有权路径上；(3)垂直切片增长协议，强制最困难优先的执行顺序；(4)漂移门，将规范-代码差异设为阻塞性合并条件。该设计综合了Parnas信息隐藏、C4、ADR、Walking Skeleton、Reflexion Models、Fitness Functions等已建立的软件工程原则。

Result: 该框架在不引入RUP或MDA等重量级框架开销的前提下，将经典软件工程原则整合为精简、代码耦合、机器可执行的统一方案，有效应对AI编码代理的两种失效模式。

Conclusion: Spec Growth Engine通过轻量级、机器可执行的规范驱动机制，解决了AI编码代理在仓库级推理和规范-代码一致性维护方面的核心挑战，为AI辅助软件开发提供了一种实用的结构化方法。

Abstract: AI coding agents dramatically accelerate implementation speed but introduce two structural failure modes that existing spec-driven approaches do not fully solve: (1) context explosion -- the agent must reason over an entire repository at once, degrading output quality as the context window fills; and (2) silent spec-code drift -- code evolves, the specification does not, and the divergence becomes invisible until it is costly to repair.
  We present the Spec Growth Engine, a lightweight framework that addresses both failure modes through a machine-readable spec graph whose nodes carry explicit contract/design separation, a Spine context assembler that scopes agent context to an ownership path, a vertical-slice growth protocol that enforces hardest-first ordering, and a drift gate that makes spec-code divergence a blocking merge condition.
  The design synthesises well-established software engineering principles (Parnas information hiding, C4, ADRs, Walking Skeleton, Reflexion Models, Fitness Functions) into a lean, code-coupled, machine-enforced whole -- without the overhead of heavy-weight frameworks such as RUP or MDA.

</details>


### [17] [ATGBuilder: Feature-Assisted Graph Learning for Activity Transition Graph Construction with Seed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27080)
*Chenhui Cui,Zixiang Xian,Danyu Li,Tao Li,Rubing Huang,Dave Towey,Shikai Guo,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATGBuilder 是一种基于特征辅助图学习的方法，利用大语言模型总结UI活动元数据，并结合微件触发信息进行边属性建模，通过辅助重建目标优化模型训练，显著提升了活动转换图的构建质量，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有活动转换图（ATG）构建方法存在局限性：静态分析可能遗漏合法转换或提取不可行转换，动态探索可能产生不完整的转换。尽管已有基于学习的方案将ATG构建视为种子监督的链接预测任务，但对活动布局和微件触发信息的使用仍然有限。

Method: ATGBuilder 采用大语言模型（LLM）从布局中总结UI活动元数据为紧凑的文本功能摘要；显式地将微件触发信息建模到边属性中；在模型训练期间引入辅助的微件属性重建目标。

Result: 在 frontmatter 语料库上的消融实验和基于人工校验的真实ATG基准测试中，ATGBuilder 显著优于现有最先进方法。进一步实验表明，ATGBuilder 通过提供更好的导航指导，提升了自动化GUI探索工具的性能。

Conclusion: ATGBuilder 通过特征辅助的图学习方法，有效利用了LLM总结的UI功能摘要和微件触发信息，在ATG构建任务上取得了显著提升，并可有效应用于自动化GUI探索的导航优化。

Abstract: Android applications are organized around activities that provide visual Graphical User Interface (GUI) containers that host the UI and handle user interaction events. Activity Transition Graphs (ATGs) have been widely used to model apps' GUI navigation. However, the construction of high-quality ATGs is challenging: ATGs based on static analysis may miss acceptable transitions and may extract infeasible ones; while dynamically explored ATGs can yield incomplete transitions. Recent learning-based approaches can treat ATG construction as a seed-supervised link-prediction task. However, the use of activity-layout and widget-trigger information for ATG construction remains limited. We propose ATGBuilder, a feature-assisted graph-learning approach for seed-supervised ATG construction. ATGBuilder uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to summarize UI activity metadata from layouts into compact textual functionality summaries. ATGBuilder explicitly models widget-trigger information into the edge attribute: It then uses an auxiliary widget-attribute reconstruction objective on this information during model training. ATGBuilder's performance was evaluated across a series of ablations on the frontmatter corpus, and an experiment on benchmark using manually-checked ground-truth ATGs. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that ATGBuilder significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. We further demonstrate its effectiveness by improving automated GUI exploration tools through better navigation guidance.

</details>


### [18] [On the Reproducibility of Quantum Software Defect Datasets: A Case Study of Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27124)
*Haruto Ohto,Yuta Ishimoto,Shinsuke Matsumoto,Shinji Kusumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Zhu等人关于软件缺陷数据集可重现性的工作进行了复制研究，聚焦于量子软件缺陷数据集Bugs4Q。实验表明，Bugs4Q在Qiskit v0.20.1上可重现性为62.2%，在v2.3.1上降至16.2%，93.6%的失败与依赖相关。与先前工作不同，大多数重现失败无法仅通过调整依赖版本解决，需修改源代码。作者据此构建了Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本，将可重现性提升至78.4%，强调了快速演化的量子软件生态中持续数据集维护的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Zhu等）已发现传统软件缺陷数据集（如Defects4J）随时间的推移会出现重现失败问题，但尚不清楚该发现是否适用于量子软件缺陷数据集。本研究旨在通过复制研究，探究量子软件缺陷数据集Bugs4Q的可重现性问题。

Method: 对Bugs4Q中37个工件在21个核心库版本上进行了77,700次量子程序执行实验，系统评估其可重现性随Qiskit版本演化的变化，并对失败根因进行人工审查。

Result: Bugs4Q可重现性从Qiskit v0.20.1的62.2%下降至v2.3.1的16.2%；93.6%的失败由依赖相关原因导致；与先前工作不同，大部分失败无法仅通过调整依赖版本解决，需进行源代码修改（如迁移导入路径和API调用）。Bugs4Q-Robust补丁版本将可重现性从16.2%提升至78.4%。

Conclusion: 研究结果与先前工作一致，但揭示了量子软件领域的独特差异——依赖版本调整不足以解决大多数重现失败，需要源代码层面的修改。这凸显了在快速演化的量子软件生态中，对缺陷数据集进行持续维护的重要性。

Abstract: The reproducibility of software defect datasets is essential for obtaining reliable and comparable research results. Zhu et al. have shown that defect datasets such as Defects4J suffer from reproduction failures (i.e., reported bugs become non-reproducible) as time passes since their creation. However, it remains unclear whether these findings generalize to quantum software defect datasets. We therefore conduct a replication study of the prior work using Bugs4Q, a widely used dataset of real-world bugs in quantum programs. Our analysis includes 77,700 quantum program executions of 37 Bugs4Q artifacts across 21 core-library versions. The experimental results showed that the reproducibility of Bugs4Q dropped from 62.2% on Qiskit v0.20.1 to 16.2% on v2.3.1, the latest version as of April 1, 2026. A manual inspection of the root causes further indicated that 93.6% of the failures were dependency-related. While these findings are consistent with those of the prior work, we also observed differences. In particular, most reproduction failures in Bugs4Q cannot be resolved merely by adjusting dependency versions; instead, they require source-code modifications such as migrating import paths and API invocations. Based on this observation, we curated Bugs4Q-Robust, a patched version of Bugs4Q to restore reproducibility. Bugs4Q-Robust increases reproducibility from 16.2% to 78.4% on Qiskit v2.3.1. Our findings highlight the importance of continuous dataset maintenance in the rapidly evolving quantum software ecosystem.

</details>


### [19] [Smaller Models, Unexpected Costs: Trade-offs in LLM Quantization for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27205)
*Fernando Vallecillos-Ruiz,Giordano d'Aloisio,Max Hort,Luca Traini,Antinisca Di Marco,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM量化在自动程序修复（APR）任务中的影响进行实证评估，分析了13种量化配置在6个LLM上的表现。研究发现：量化模型与基础模型修复的问题集重叠度低但数量相当；量化虽能减少最多85%的内存占用，却增加了推理时间和能耗（归因于硬件利用次优）；Pareto分析显示48%的配置被严格支配；量化效果对模型架构和任务复杂度高度敏感，不存在普适的最优量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅用基准分数概括量化效果，掩盖了量化对模型行为和非功能开销（如能耗、推理时间）的实际影响，缺乏在复杂软件工程任务（如APR）中对量化进行全面评估的工作。

Method: 选取6个代表性LLM，在HumanEval-Java和Defects4J两个APR基准上测试13种量化配置（不同位宽、方法、目标组件如权重和KV缓存），从修复效果、内存占用、推理时间、能耗等多个维度进行对比和Pareto分析。

Result: 量化模型与基础模型修复的问题重叠度低但修复数量相当；内存占用最高减少85%；推理时间和能耗反而增加；48%的配置在Pareto分析中被严格支配；量化效果依赖模型架构和任务复杂度。

Conclusion: 不存在通用的最优量化方法，量化决策需在有效性、内存和能效之间权衡，且高度依赖模型架构和任务特性；建议未来量化评估应纳入更全面的多维度指标。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) are powerful toolsand have been increasingly adopted for complex software engineering tasks. As the number of parameters increases, results can often be improved, but this also imposes substantialmemory requirements. While quantization effectively reduces thememory footprint, its overall impact is often summarized onlyby benchmark scores, which mask changes in model behaviorand non-functional overheads. In this work, we conduct anempirical evaluation of LLM quantization using AutomatedProgram Repair (APR), a complex task in software engineering.We analyze 13 quantization configurations spanning differentbit-widths, methods, and target components (weights and KVcache) across six representative LLMs, evaluated on two APRbenchmarks (HumanEval-Java and Defects4J).
  Our findings reveal that base and quantized models can provide different sets of repaired problems with little overlap, whileretaining a comparable number of repaired problems. Althoughquantization successfully reduces memory footprints by up to85%, it increases both inference time and energy consumption,which we attribute to suboptimal hardware utilization. OurPareto trade-off analysis shows that 48% of the configurationsevaluated are strictly dominated by alternatives. Rather thanidentifying a superior quantization method, our findings highlightthat the trade-offs between effectiveness, memory footprint,and energy efficiency are sensitive to the underlying modelarchitecture and the complexity of the task.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27258)
*Daniel Jackson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判了面向对象编程的核心原则——将系统功能按问题域中的个体进行划分，指出这一原则导致功能碎片化和混淆，并提出放弃面向对象，转而采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向对象编程的核心原则（按问题域个体划分功能）已主导软件开发五十余年，但后续软件工程方法试图解决的功能碎片化和混淆问题，实际上正是这一原则的必然结果，因此需要从根本上重新审视。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法，批判性地审视面向对象的核心原则及其后果，并提出一种替代方案——将问题域中的个体与划分功能的模块解耦。

Result: 研究表明，面向对象的核心原则并非自然或直接的，后续方法试图缓解的问题（功能碎片化和混淆）正是该原则的必然产物。

Conclusion: 解决这些问题的更简单路径是放弃面向对象，采用将问题域个体与功能模块解耦的替代方法。

Abstract: A core principle of object orientation -- that the functionality of a system can be partitioned amongst objects that correspond to individuals in the problem domain -- has influenced how software has been specified, designed and implemented for more than fifty years. Later developments in software engineering sought to build on this principle. But in fact this partitioning is neither natural nor straightforward, and the problems that these later developments sought to mitigate -- the fragmentation and conflation of functionality -- were often, in fact, the inevitable consequences of this founding principle. An easier path to addressing these problems therefore starts by going back, abandoning object orientation, and replacing it with an alternative approach that decouples the individuals of the problem domain from the modules that partition functionality.

</details>

### [21] [Reducing Redundancy in Whole-Slide Image Patching for Scalable Indexing and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26157)
*Jialiang Geng,Ghazal Alabtah,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ARReST（反冗余缩减策略），通过识别并剪除跨类别区分贡献最小的对抗性病理切片，在保持检索性能的同时实现全切片图像索引的压缩，存储节省3%-60%（平均14%±13%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理学的快速发展对全切片图像的高效索引与检索提出了迫切需求，尤其是检索增强生成等生成式AI工作流需要可靠的相似性搜索支持临床决策。然而高性能存储的高昂成本限制了WSI索引的可扩展性和可及性，亟需一种能降低存储需求同时保持检索精度的方法。

Method: 提出ARReST（反冗余缩减策略），一种基于对抗性原理的框架。不同于仅消除类内重复，ARReST识别那些对跨类别区分贡献最小的对抗性切片（antithetical patches），并将其从可搜索存档中剪除，从而在保留形态多样性和检索保真度的前提下大幅压缩索引。

Result: 在TCGA数据集（涵盖21个器官）上的实验表明，ARReST实现了显著的索引压缩，存储节省范围为3%至60%（平均14%±13%），且对多个器官的检索性能无负面影响。

Conclusion: ARReST策略实现了可扩展、成本高效的WSI索引，适用于下一代基于检索的临床AI系统，特别适合需要高保真相似性搜索的RAG等生成式AI工作流。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital pathology has created an urgent need for efficient indexing and retrieval of whole slide images (WSIs). This need is intensified by emerging generative AI workflows, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which require dependable similarity search to support high-stakes clinical decision-making. Yet the substantial cost of high-performance storage limits the scalability and accessibility of WSI indexing for many healthcare institutions. Consequently, methods that can reduce storage demands while preserving retrieval accuracy have become a critical research priority. We propose ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy), a principled oppositional framework that leverages redundancy across dissimilar tissue classes to markedly decrease the number of patches that must be indexed from each WSI. Instead of eliminating only within-class duplicates, ARReST identifies antithetical patches-those whose representations contribute minimally to cross-class discrimination-and prunes them from the searchable archive. This targeted reduction substantially compresses the index without sacrificing morphological diversity or retrieval fidelity. By minimizing superfluous patch representations, ARReST reduces storage footprint, lowers computational overhead, and accelerates similarity search across large pathology repositories. Extensive experiments on TCGA repository (The Cancer Genome Atlas with 21 organs) demonstrate that ARReST achieves significant index compression while maintaining competitive retrieval performance. The observed storage savings of 3% to 60% (14%$\pm$13%) can be reliably achieved without compromising retrieval performance for many organs. The proposed strategy enables scalable, cost-efficient WSI indexing and is well-suited for next-generation retrieval-driven clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [22] [From Clicks to Intent: Cross-Platform Session Embeddings with LLM-Distilled Taxonomy for Financial Services Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26277)
*Dianjing Fan,Yao Li,Kyaw Hpone Myint,Dwipam Katariya,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的双用途意图预测框架，将网络点击流通过自监督Transformer编码为会话嵌入以支持定量推荐，同时利用LLM生成可解释的意图标签，在金融服务的跨渠道个性化场景中显著提升了推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融服务场景中，登录前网页浏览与登录后应用内行为存在巨大差异（探索新产品 vs. 账户服务），且跨渠道实体解析困难导致网页端意图信号未被充分利用。现有方法缺乏灵活性和可扩展性，无法同时支持定量推荐和定性理解。

Method: 提出双用途框架：1) 自监督Transformer将多模态点击流编码为紧凑会话嵌入；2) LLM驱动的分类法生成与蒸馏管道产出可解释意图标签。两部分协同服务于定量任务与定性理解。

Result: 移动端首页磁贴排序任务：会话嵌入在宏Recall@1上提升1.88%，Log Loss降低13.38%。用户转化预测任务：嵌入在微F1上优于LLM标签4.3%；蒸馏层以超低延迟交付可解释标签，性能仅下降7%。

Conclusion: 自监督点击流表示与LLM蒸馏分类法相结合，能够在工业级生产环境中同时高效支持定量排序任务与可解释意图理解，证明了跨渠道个性化在金融服务中的可行性。

Abstract: Sequential user behavior modeling is widely adopted in industrial recommender systems; however, significant gaps remain in financial services, where pre-login web interactions and authenticated in-app experiences differ drastically. Specifically, pre-login web users typically explore new products, whereas logged-in app users focus on account servicing. Due to the challenge of cross-channel entity resolution (e.g., matching anonymous web sessions to authenticated mobile accounts), web-based intent signals remain underutilized for post-authentication personalization. Existing methods for capturing web-based intent are often ad-hoc and narrow, lacking the flexibility to support both quantitative downstream recommendations and qualitative understanding at scale. In this work, we propose a scalable and dual-purpose intent prediction framework for web-based interactions and demonstrate its applicability for personalization. Our approach transforms raw web clickstreams into two outputs: a self-supervised Transformer encodes multi-modal clickstreams into a compact session embedding, while an LLM-based taxonomy generation and distillation pipeline produces interpretable intent labels. Our system demonstrates that self-supervised clickstream representations combined with LLM-distilled taxonomies can jointly serve quantitative tasks and qualitative understanding in production: on the mobile homepage tile ranking task, the session embedding improves macro Recall@1 by 1.88% and reduces Log Loss by 13.38% over production baselines. On the user conversion prediction task, the embedding outperforms the LLM labels by 4.3% on micro F1, while the distillation layer delivers interpretable labels at ultra-low latency with only a 7% performance drop.

</details>


### [23] [Scoring Is Not Enough: Addressing Gaps in Utility-fairness Trade-offs for Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26369)
*Shubham Singh,Ian A. Kash,Mesrob I. Ohannessian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在排序任务中，仅使用评分函数（scoring functions）无法实现效用与公平性的最优权衡，而半贪婪后处理方法能够接近理想效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算法公平性研究的兴起，现有的评分学习方法试图同时优化效用和公平性，但本文质疑评分函数是否足以实现两者之间的最优权衡。

Method: 通过一系列反例，在通用公平性公式下展示评分函数的次优性，并考虑确定性/随机化评分、单查询/多查询公平性度量等不同设置。正面贡献是提出半贪婪后处理方法。

Result: 评分函数在所有考察的设置下均无法达到效用-公平性的最优权衡；半贪婪后处理方法能够以可计算的方式接近穷举后处理的理想权衡。

Conclusion: 评分函数在兼顾公平与效用时存在根本性局限，后处理（尤其是半贪婪方法）是更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Scoring functions are used to represent the relevance of individual documents. In modern information retrieval or recommendation systems, they are often learned from data and play a pivotal role in ranking sets of documents or items in a way that maximizes utility to a query or user. With the recent interest in algorithmic fairness, the success of scoring has naturally led to methods that learn scores that simultaneously trade off fairness and utility. In this work, we show that in stark contrast with utility-centric objectives, scoring is sub-optimal in achieving all utility-fairness trade-offs. We establish this with a series of counter-examples with a generic fairness formulation. We show that the issue persists whether we have a deterministic scoring function or a randomized one, or whether we measure fairness at the scope of a single query or across multiple queries. On the positive side, we empirically demonstrate that semi-greedy post-processing has the potential to achieve much better trade-offs, often approaching the ideal of exhaustive post-processing in a tractable way.

</details>


### [24] [TileMaxSim: IO-Aware GPU MaxSim Scoring with Dimension Tiling and Fused Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26439)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TileMaxSim，一种IO感知的Triton核函数族，通过多查询SRAM分块、维度分块和融合乘积量化评分技术，将MaxSim在GPU上的HBM带宽利用率从5-18%提升至80.2%，达到82M文档/秒的评分吞吐量，在ColBERTv2/PLAID中将端到端延迟降低98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型（如ColBERT）依赖细粒度token级MaxSim评分实现SOTA精度，但现有GPU实现因物化Nq×Nd相似度矩阵导致严重的内存带宽瓶颈，仅达到峰值HBM带宽的5-18%，大量算力资源被浪费。

Method: (1) 多查询SRAM分块：将文档嵌入流式通过共享内存，同时累加每个查询token的最大值到寄存器，使每个嵌入仅从HBM读取一次；(2) 维度分块：将嵌入维度划分为128宽的块，支持d>128的嵌入评分；(3) 融合乘积量化评分：通过共享内存查找表减少HBM I/O达~31倍。

Result: NVIDIA H100上达到80.2%峰值HBM带宽，82M文档/秒（MS MARCO上71.6M/s），相对循环评分加速220倍，相对融合PyTorch加速6.5倍，相对torch.compile加速6.6-8.5倍，相对WARP CPU引擎加速469倍；ColBERTv2/PLAID中100K候选延迟从268ms降至1.2ms（降低98%）。

Conclusion: TileMaxSim在保持精确检索质量的前提下，通过IO感知的核函数设计彻底解决了MaxSim评分的内存带宽瓶颈，实现了两个数量级的性能提升，可作为ColBERTv2/PLAID的即插即用替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art accuracy through fine-grained token-level MaxSim scoring, yet existing GPU implementations leave most hardware performance unused. We give a roofline analysis of MaxSim on modern GPUs and identify a severe bandwidth gap: naive implementations reach only 5-18% of peak HBM bandwidth because they materialize the Nq x Nd similarity matrix, wasting memory traffic on data that is consumed once and discarded. We present TileMaxSim, a family of IO-aware Triton kernels that close this gap via (1) multi-query SRAM tiling that streams document embeddings through shared memory while accumulating per-query-token maxima in registers, reading each embedding from HBM exactly once; (2) dimension tiling that partitions the embedding dimension into 128-wide chunks, enabling scoring for d > 128 embeddings that overflow shared memory; and (3) fused product-quantization scoring via shared-memory lookup tables, cutting HBM I/O by up to ~31x. On NVIDIA H100 GPUs, TileMaxSim reaches 80.2% of peak HBM bandwidth and scores 82M documents/second (71.6M/s on real MS MARCO passages), a 220x speedup over loop-based scoring, 6.5x over fused PyTorch, 6.6-8.5x over torch.compile, and 469x the scoring throughput of WARP's CPU engine on the same node. TileMaxSim preserves exact retrieval quality: on MS MARCO and three BEIR benchmarks, rankings match reference MaxSim. As a drop-in replacement in ColBERTv2/PLAID, it cuts scoring latency at 100K candidates from 268 ms to 1.2 ms (98% lower end-to-end latency). We further show constant throughput from 100K to 500K documents, data-parallel multi-GPU sharding, robustness across dimensions 64-768, and FP16/BF16/FP32 support. Concurrent work independently develops an IO-aware fused MaxSim kernel; we differ in dimension tiling for d > 128 and fused product-quantization scoring.

</details>


### [25] [GPUSparse: GPU-Accelerated Learned Sparse Retrieval with Parallel Inverted Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26441)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了GPUSparse系统，通过GPU加速实现精确的稀疏检索，在MS MARCO数据集上达到与CPU精确评分相同的效果，同时提供235倍速度提升（1.27ms vs 298ms每查询），并分析了工作效率与硬件利用率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型稀疏检索模型（如SPLADE）虽然检索质量高且具有可解释性优势，但推理阶段的评分仍然依赖CPU上的倒排索引遍历算法（WAND、Block-Max WAND），这成为大规模实时服务的基础瓶颈。

Method: (1) GPU并行倒排索引，包含块对齐、warp合并的倒排列表；(2) 批量scatter-add评分算法，可同时处理数百个查询；(3) 融合Triton内核，分析工作效率与硬件利用率之间的权衡。

Result: 在MS MARCO passage ranking（880万段落）上使用真实SPLADE嵌入：MRR@10=0.383（与Pyserini SPLADE精度一致）；Recall@1000>=0.999；相比Pyserini CPU实现235倍加速（1.27ms vs 298ms每查询）；在880万完整集合上达到787 QPS吞吐量（batch 500），每查询1.3ms；文档并行内核达到H100峰值HBM带宽的62.6%。

Conclusion: 将稀疏评分重新表述为倒排索引上的scatter-add操作，并通过融合内核实现，相比SPARe的迭代式实现快23-270倍。揭示了GPU稀疏检索中工作效率与带宽效率之间的根本性权衡。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE achieve retrieval quality competitive with dense models while preserving the interpretability and exact-match advantages of sparse representations. However, inference-time scoring still relies on CPU-bound inverted index traversal algorithms (WAND, Block-Max WAND), creating a fundamental bottleneck for real-time serving at scale. We present GPUSparse, a system for GPU-accelerated exact learned sparse retrieval that introduces: (1) a GPU-parallel inverted index with block-aligned, warp-coalesced posting lists; (2) a batched scatter-add scoring algorithm that processes hundreds of queries simultaneously; and (3) fused Triton kernels with an analysis of the tradeoff between work-efficiency and hardware utilization. On MS MARCO passage ranking (8.8M passages) with real SPLADE embeddings, GPUSparse matches CPU exact scoring to three decimals (MRR@10=0.383, equal to Pyserini SPLADE at this precision; Recall@1000>=0.999 vs. dense matmul, the residual from floating-point tie-breaking) while providing a 235x speedup over Pyserini CPU at 8.8M documents (1.27ms vs. 298ms per query). Compared to Seismic (the fastest CPU sparse retrieval system), which trades 25% recall for speed (R@1000=0.738 vs. 0.983 exact), GPUSparse achieves exact scoring at 787 QPS throughput (batch 500) on the full 8.8M collection, with 1.3ms per query. Our document-parallel kernel reaches 62.6% of H100 peak HBM bandwidth, revealing a fundamental work-efficiency vs. bandwidth-efficiency tradeoff in GPU sparse retrieval. The reformulation of sparse scoring as scatter-add over an inverted index is shared with SPARe's iterative mode; our contribution is its fused-kernel realization, which we measure to be 23-270x faster than a faithful SPARe iterative reimplementation.

</details>


### [26] [Attributed, But Not Incremental: Cannibalization-Corrected Attribution for Large-Scale Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26690)
*Donghui Li,Bowen Yuan,Zili Yang,Qinxin Chen,Lijing Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实验校准的归因校正框架，利用增量实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏的提升测量转化为每日校正估计，并在业务层级下分配校准后的蚕食量。在TikTok多市场部署后，蚕食率降低了约15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模付费获客系统中，归因输出（如DNU）可能因付费渠道与自然需求、品牌流量或其他渠道重叠而系统性高估真实增量增长，导致增量ROI测量失真和预算决策偏差。

Method: 提出实验校准的归因校正框架：1）以增量实验作为因果锚点，将稀疏的提升测量转换为每日校正估计；2）在结构性一致性约束下，将校准后的蚕食量分配至业务层级。

Result: 离线前向时间序列验证表明，相比原始归因和细粒度ML基线，框架显著降低了校准误差；在TikTok全球多市场部署后，蚕食率降低约15个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验校准的归因校正框架能有效解决归因与蚕食之间的不匹配问题，改善增量ROI测量和预算决策，且已在大规模工业系统中验证有效。

Abstract: In large-scale paid acquisition and growth advertising systems, production attribution outputs are widely used for daily budget allocation and channel diagnosis. However, paid-attributed conversions such as daily new users (DNU) may systematically overstate true incremental growth when paid channels overlap with organic demand, brand-driven traffic, or other acquisition channels. This attribution-cannibalization mismatch can distort incremental ROI measurement and budget decisions at scale.
  We propose an experiment-calibrated attribution correction framework that uses incrementality experiments as causal anchors to convert sparse lift measurements into daily correction estimates. To make the corrected signal actionable at production granularity, we further allocate calibrated cannibalization volume across business hierarchies under structural consistency constraints. Offline forward-in-time validation against channel-level incrementality experiment readouts shows that the proposed framework substantially reduces calibration error relative to raw attribution and fine-grained ML baselines. Deployed across multiple global TikTok markets, the system supported budget and traffic strategy adjustments that were followed by an approximately 15-percentage-point reduction in the measured cannibalization rate.

</details>


### [27] [A Shared IPTC Topic Space for Cross-Source Topic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26845)
*Din Iskakov,Sebastian Gonçalves,Marco Idiat,Mendeli Vainstein,Aline Villavicencio,Ronaldo Menezes,Rodrigo Wilkens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的框架，利用IPTC媒体主题分类法将不同语料库映射到统一的共享主题空间，通过引导式BERTopic和加权关键词-目标质心评分实现跨源主题比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同媒体间的主题注意力比较受限于一个根本的建模问题：分别拟合各语料库的主题模型会产生无法直接对齐的语料库特有主题空间。

Method: 采用引导式BERTopic发现主题，通过加权关键词和目标质心对94个IPTC一级主题进行评分，再通过最大相似度规则向上合并至17个IPTC父级主题。框架开发基于NYT 2011语料库，经过模型筛选、映射优化、终选比较、目标消融和阈值校准五个阶段。

Result: 在NYT 2011语料库中，引导族在更严格的分配阈值下保留了比零样本基准更强的映射覆盖率；父级富化的目标构建提高了覆盖率和父级一致性；覆盖率随阈值收紧而逐渐下降而非崩溃。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提供了一种外部锚定的方法，用于构建共享主题空间，从而能够实现可复现的跨源主题比较。

Abstract: Comparing topic attention across different media is hindered by a fundamental modelling problem: topic models fitted separately to each corpus produce corpus-specific topic spaces that cannot be aligned directly. This paper presents a reproducible framework that places corpora in a single shared topic space defined by a taxonomy. Discovered topics are obtained with guided BERTopic, scored against the ninety-four IPTC Media Topics' taxonomy topics (level-1) through weighted keyword and target centroids, and then collapsed upward to seventeen IPTC parent topics by a maximum-similarity rule. The framework was developed and selected on a controlled New York Times 2011 corpus through a narrowing sequence: a broad model screen, a focused mapping refinement, a strict finalist comparison, a target-construction ablation, and a threshold calibration. In this corpus, the guided family retained substantially stronger mapped coverage than a zero-shot benchmark under stricter assignment thresholds, a parent-enriched target construction improved both coverage and parent consistency, and coverage declined gradually rather than collapsing as the assignment threshold was tightened. The contribution is an externally anchored method for constructing a shared topic space that enables reproducible cross-source topic comparison.

</details>


### [28] [TriPAH: Imbalance-Aware Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing for Cross-Modal Medical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27010)
*Jiaming Bian,Songming Li,Yurui Song,Yunfei Chen,Yichao Cao,Jun Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TriPAH框架，通过三提示亲和哈希实现跨模态医学检索，利用本体引导的患者级提示、轻量提示-令牌混合器和患者级一致性模块，在三个公开数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态医学哈希检索方法受临床语言噪声、长尾标签和脆弱量化导致的语义碎片化问题困扰，弱化了图像-文本语义对齐。

Method: TriPAH包含三个核心组件：1）本体引导的患者级提示生成低噪声文本表征进行初始对齐；2）轻量提示-令牌混合器实现层级多粒度对齐，在非对称多任务目标下结合多正例对比对齐、不平衡感知分类和渐进量化正则化；3）患者级一致性模块稳定互补视图下的哈希码。

Result: 在三个公开医学数据集上，TriPAH显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过提示驱动和渐进量化策略，TriPAH有效缓解了临床语义碎片化问题，提升了跨模态医学检索性能，可支持基于病例的推理和决策支持等下游任务。

Abstract: In the era of big medical data, efficient cross-modal retrieval is pivotal for evidence-based diagnosis and large-scale case management. Cross-modal medical hashing retrieval aims to enable efficient image-text search and support downstream tasks such as case-based reasoning and decision support by learning compact, semantically aligned binary codes. However, current methods suffer from semantic fragmentation due to noisy clinical language, long-tailed labels, and brittle quantization that weakens alignment. We propose TriPAH, a Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing framework. TriPAH synthesizes ontology-grounded, patient-level prompts conditioned on normalized clinical cues to yield low-noise textual representations for initial alignment. A lightweight prompt-token mixer performs hierarchical, multi-granularity alignment and produces quantization-ready features under an asymmetric multi-task objective coupling multi-positive contrastive alignment, imbalance-aware classification, and progressive quantization regularization. A patient-level consistency module further stabilizes codes across complementary views. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that TriPAH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [29] [UniFormer: Efficient and Unified Model-Centric Scaling for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27058)
*Bo Chen,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Ruihao Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Chenghou Jin,Qinglin Jia,Baixuan He,Hechang Pan,Yiwu Liu,Jian Liang,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniFormer，一种面向工业推荐系统的统一模型中心缩放框架。通过将建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间，引入语义分词实现用户-物品解耦以加速推理，采用多序列交叉注意力防止偏好坍塌，并设计多视图FFN支持灵活参数缩放。在快手和快手极速版上的在线A/B测试中，UniFormer在App停留时间和观看时长上分别取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统主要采用组件中心缩放（独立缩放行为建模、特征交互或任务建模等组件），而HyFormer、OneTrans等方法虽尝试跨模块协同缩放，但设计仍局限于特征空间，缺乏统一的模型中心缩放框架，无法覆盖整体建模空间。

Method: 1) 将整体建模空间分解为特征空间和任务空间，分别由堆叠的Feature-space Interaction Modules和Task-space Interaction Modules建模；2) 引入基于语义的分词方案实现用户-物品解耦，支持请求级推理加速；3) 采用多序列交叉注意力分别捕获异质行为模式，配合自注意力增强交互建模；4) 设计多视图FFN支持灵活可扩展的参数缩放。

Result: 在快手和快手极速版两个生产场景的在线A/B测试中：App停留时间分别提升+0.101%/+0.260%，观看时长分别提升+0.729%/+1.113%。

Conclusion: UniFormer通过统一的模型中心缩放框架，在工业推荐系统中实现了效率与性能的双重提升，验证了将建模空间分解为特征与任务空间的有效性，为推荐系统的大规模模型缩放提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recently, substantial progress has been made in industrial recommendation through component-centric model scaling, where individual components such as behavior modeling, feature interaction, or task modeling are independently scaled to improve model capacity. Although recent methods such as HyFormer and OneTrans further explore cross-module co-scaling by jointly modeling behavior and interaction, their designs are still confined to the feature space and lack a unified model-centric scaling framework over the overall modeling space. In this paper, we propose UniFormer, an efficient and unified model-centric scaling framework for industrial recommender systems. To improve efficiency, UniFormer decomposes the overall modeling space into feature and task spaces, which are modeled by stacked Feature-space Interaction Modules and Task-space Interaction Modules, respectively. Moreover, UniFormer introduces semantic-based tokenization scheme to enable user-item decoupling, thereby achieving request-level inference acceleration. To prevent preference collapse, UniFormer employs multi-sequence cross-attention to separately capture heterogeneous behavior patterns, followed by the self-attention to enhance interaction modeling. Besides, dedicated multi-view FFNs are introduced to support flexible and scalable parameter scaling across different modeling components. Extensive online A/B testing in two production scenarios, Kuaishou and Kuaishou Lite, shows that UniFormer consistently improves user engagement and interaction metrics, achieving gains of +0.101%/+0.260% in App Stay Time and +0.729%/+1.113% in Watch Time, respectively.

</details>


### [30] [TRUST: Item-Calibrated Interval Evidence for Temporal Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27214)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Yifan Yin,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST框架，发现现有时间会话推荐中"相同时间间隔在不同物品上含义相同"的假设不成立，通过将时间间隔相对于物品自身分布进行校准，在邻居采样、会话图编码和兴趣聚合三个模块中提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间会话推荐模型隐式假设相同的时间间隔在不同物品上承载相似的兴趣信号，但经验发现每个物品有其自身的时间间隔分布，相同间隔在不同物品上的含义不同，因此需要对时间信号进行物品级别的相对校准。

Method: 提出TRUST框架：1) 设计评分函数，将观测到的时间间隔相对于对应物品的经验间隔分布进行校准；2) 将校准后的分数用于指导全局邻居采样、会话图编码和最终兴趣聚合三个模块。

Result: 在公开数据集上，TRUST一致优于代表性的时间和非时间基线方法；作为模型无关的插件方法，评分函数能提升现有的时间会话推荐器；消融实验证明在每个模块内校准时间信号而非移除模块，能持续改进性能。

Conclusion: 将时间信号相对于物品自身分布进行校准确认是更合理的时间建模方式，TRUST框架及其评分函数可作为通用组件增强现有时间会话推荐系统。

Abstract: Temporal signals have been widely used in session-based recommendation to infer user interest. Existing temporal session-based recommenders primarily rely on absolute interval values, implicitly assuming that the same interval carries similar interest signals across items. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not hold: each item has its own interval distribution, so an interval should be interpreted relative to the item it belongs to. Based on this observation, we propose TRUST, a framework that evaluates each observed interval relative to the empirical interval distribution of the corresponding item. Specifically, we propose a score function to guide global neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and final interest aggregation. Experiments on public datasets show that TRUST consistently improves over representative temporal and non-temporal baselines, and plug-in experiments further show that the proposed scoring function can improve existing temporal session recommenders as a model-agnostic method. Component-wise ablations further show that calibrating the temporal signals within each module, rather than removing the module itself, consistently improves neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and interest aggregation.

</details>


### [31] [NOVA: A Verification-Aware Agent Harness for Architecture Evolution in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27243)
*Shaohua Liu,Liang Fang,Yilong Sun,Shudong Huang,Qingsong Luo,Xiaoyang Chen,Dongqiang Liu,Chuangang Ma,Zhenzhen Chai,Henghuan Wang,Shijie Quan,Changyuan Cui,Zhangbin Zhu,Peng Chen,Wei Xu,Lei Xiao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NOVA是一个面向工业广告推荐系统架构演进的层级感知智能体框架。它通过架构梯度（受SGD启发的不可微更新信号）和验证级联（从结构语义到在线影响的多级检查）来引导架构修改，并结合L1-L4任务级控制自动化风险。在工业广告系统中部署后，NOVA在L2和L3任务上取得了最高有效通过率（54.5%和60.0%），将文献到生产的周期缩短了13倍以上，并在在线A/B测试中使三个pCVR目标的GMV提升了+1.25%、+1.70%和+2.02%，同时pCVR偏差降低了58.8%、66.7%和37.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型的架构演进虽然能带来显著的业务增益（如RankMixer、TokenMixer-Large、MixFormer），但开发这些升级是专家密集型且难以规模化的。现有自动化方法（AutoML调超参、通用LLM编码智能体优化可运行代码）不足以应对跨模块架构变更的严格约束，候选架构可能通过本地测试但在线上产生静默失败。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，包含三个核心组件：1）架构梯度（Architecture Gradient），一种受SGD启发的不可微更新信号，聚合先前的修改、验证诊断、指标反馈和轨迹记忆来引导下一步修改；2）验证级联（Verification Cascade），依次检查结构语义、本地可执行性、离线有效性和在线影响，早期阻断无效候选并记录失败模式为禁止方向；3）L1-L4任务级控制（Task-Level Control），根据任务复杂度和风险匹配自动化程度，高风险任务路由给Copilot进行人工监督。

Result: 在工业广告系统中，NOVA在L2 ScaleUp和L3 Literature-to-Production任务上分别达到54.5%和60.0%的最高有效通过率，相比编码智能体基线减少了静默失败，将文献到生产周期中的人工参与时间缩短了13倍以上。在线A/B测试中，选中的L3候选架构在三个pCVR目标上分别提升GMV +1.25%、+1.70%、+2.02%，同时pCVR偏差降低58.8%、66.7%、37.3%。

Conclusion: NOVA通过层级感知的智能体框架有效解决了工业推荐系统架构演进的自动化难题，验证了结合架构梯度、验证级联和任务级控制的方法能够实现高效的、可规模的架构演进，同时显著降低人工成本并提升业务指标。

Abstract: Industrial advertising recommender models are continuously improved through architecture evolution. Upgrades such as RankMixer, TokenMixer-Large, and MixFormer show that better structures remain a key source of quality and business gains. Yet developing such upgrades in production is expert-intensive and difficult to scale. Existing automation is insufficient: AutoML mainly tunes hyper-parameters, while effective gains often require cross-module changes under strict constraints; generic LLM coding agents optimize for runnable code, but runnable code does not imply a valid recommender architecture. Candidates may pass local tests while causing silent failures that degrade performance.
  We present NOVA, a level-aware agent harness for verification-aware architecture evolution. NOVA uses an architecture gradient, an SGD-inspired, non-differentiable update signal that aggregates prior modifications, verification diagnostics, metric feedback, and trajectory memory to guide the next modification. A verification cascade checks structure semantics, local executability, offline effectiveness, and online impact; invalid candidates are blocked early, with failure patterns recorded as forbidden directions. L1--L4 task-level control matches automation to task complexity and risk, routing high-risk tasks to Copilot for human oversight. Deployed in an industrial advertising system, NOVA achieves the highest effective pass rate on L2 ScaleUp and L3 Literature-to-Production tasks (54.5% and 60.0%), reduces silent failures compared with coding-agent baselines, and shortens one literature-to-production cycle by over 13x in human-attended time. In online A/B testing, the selected L3 candidate improves GMV on three pCVR objectives by +1.25%, +1.70%, and +2.02%, while reducing pCVR bias by 58.8%, 66.7%, and 37.3%.

</details>

### [32] [\chisao{}: A GPU-Native Parallel Optimizer for Multimodal Black-Box Functions via Convergence-Anticonvergence Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26164)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出χισω（CHISAO），一种GPU原生群体优化器，通过收敛-反收敛振荡机制实现多模态黑盒函数的全模式检测。在SFU基准测试42个函数（维度2-64）上，该方法在CPU基线方法失败的模态函数上实现100%模式恢复，速度提升最高达39倍，且在高噪声环境下仍保持100%可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态优化方法（盆地跳跃、CMA-ES、多起点梯度下降）顺序执行，无法利用现代GPU的大规模并行能力，在高维复杂多模态函数上效率低下甚至失效。

Method: 采用GPU原生群体优化策略，整批样本并行运行。通过有意的收敛-反收敛振荡循环：达到真实峰值的样本被"冻结"保留，其余样本通过基于动量的反收敛和随机平滑梯度持续探索。结合两种自适应重采样策略（Repulse Monkey和Golden Rooster）维持种群多样性。

Result: 在SFU基准测试全部42个函数（d=2,4,8,16,32,64）上，对最难的多模态函数（CPU基线在d≥8时全部失效）实现100%模式恢复；在Michalewicz函数d=64上速度提升34倍，在Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid d=64上速度提升39倍；噪声标准差达1.0时仍100%可靠。

Conclusion: χισω证明了GPU原生群体优化器在并行多模态优化中的巨大潜力，通过刻意设计的振荡机制结合冻结策略，实现了超越传统CPU方法的性能和鲁棒性，且作为开源Python包发布。

Abstract: Finding all modes of a multimodal black-box function is a fundamental challenge in optimization, Bayesian inference, and scientific computing. Existing approaches -- basin-hopping, CMA-ES, multistart gradient descent -- operate sequentially and cannot exploit the massive parallelism of modern GPU hardware. We introduce \chisao{} (\textbf{C}onvergence-\textbf{H}alt-\textbf{I}nvert-\textbf{S}tick-\textbf{A}nd-\textbf{O}scillate), a GPU-native population optimizer that runs an entire sample batch simultaneously and exploits a deliberate convergence-anticonvergence oscillation cycle to escape local traps while freezing confirmed modes. The structural move is asymmetric: samples that reach true peaks are frozen (``stuck'') and preserved, while the rest keep exploring via momentum-based anti-convergence and stochastically smoothed gradients. Adaptive reseeding via two complementary strategies (Repulse Monkey and Golden Rooster) maintains population diversity throughout. On all 42 functions of the Simon Fraser University optimization benchmark suite across dimensions $d \in \{2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64\}$, \chisao{} achieves \textbf{100\%} mode recovery where all CPU baselines collapse at $d \geq 8$ on the hardest multimodal functions, at up to \textbf{$34\times$} speedup over basin-hopping on functions where all methods succeed (Michalewicz $d=64$) and up to \textbf{$39\times$} on unimodal functions (Rotated Hyper-Ellipsoid $d=64$, pure GPU dividend). All benchmarks evaluate the objective by value alone -- gradients come from finite differences -- so the reported speedups are a derivative-free worst case. Under substantial likelihood noise ($σ_{\mathrm{noise}}$ up to 1.0), mode detection remains 100\% reliable. The algorithm is available as a standalone open-source Python package on PyPI.

</details>
